Bible Quiz Questions – Old Testament – Volume 4: Wisdom and Psalms
Job–Psalms · 192 chapters · 1536 questions
Collection index and Volume 1
Each chapter has eight questions: three Easy, three Medium and two Hard. The question order and multiple-choice answer positions are mixed. Difficulty is editorial: Hard questions require closer recall, comparison of passages or careful interpretation.
Quotation questions marked WEB use the World English Bible. Other questions preserve the study material’s wording or paraphrase a passage. Accept a clearly equivalent answer when exact wording is not being tested.
Read the passage in context before applying it today or connecting it to Christ. A character’s statement is not automatically the narrator’s conclusion. Theological questions use the course summaries listed below alongside Scripture; disputed readings are identified in the explanations.
Sources: Old testament course summary · Gospel Worldview course summary · World English Bible
Job
Job 1
Q1. True or false: Satan challenged God about Job's faithfulness. (1:9-11) Easy
Answer: True. Job 1:9 records: “Then Satan answered Yahweh, and said, "Does Job fear God for nothing?”
Q2. Multiple choice: What does the opening tell the reader that Job's later friends do not know? (1:1, 6-12) Hard
A) Job's suffering is not introduced as punishment for a hidden offense identified by the narrator. B) Job is introduced as secretly corrupt. C) The friends observe the heavenly conversation. D) Job is punished for refusing to offer sacrifices.
Answer: A. Job's suffering is not introduced as punishment for a hidden offense identified by the narrator.. The narrator calls Job upright and describes a heavenly challenge. The book does not allow every case of suffering to be explained by blaming the sufferer.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… a day when his sons and his daughters were eating and drinking milk in their eldest brother's house,” (1:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… a day when his sons and his daughters were eating and drinking wine in their eldest brother's house,” It says wine, not milk.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Yahweh said to Satan, "Have you considered my ______, Job? For there is none like him in the earth, a blameless and an upright man, one …”? (1:8, WEB) Medium
A) servant B) judge C) husbands D) sisters
Answer: A. servant. The passage specifies servant: “Yahweh said to Satan, "Have you considered my servant, Job? For there is none like him in the earth, a blameless and an upright man, one …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “______ he was still speaking, there came also another, and said, "The Chaldeans made three bands, and swept down on the camels, and have taken them …” (1:17); “… behold, there came a great wind from the wilderness, and struck the ______ corners of the house, and it fell on the young men, and they …” (1:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: While; four. The first passage reads “While he was still speaking, there came also another, and said, "The Chaldeans made three bands, and swept down on the camels, and have taken them …”; the second reads “… behold, there came a great wind from the wilderness, and struck the four corners of the house, and it fell on the young men, and they …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… up early in the morning, and offered burnt ______ according to the number of them all. For ______ said, "It may be that my sons have …” (1:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: offerings; Job. The wording is “… up early in the morning, and offered burnt offerings according to the number of them all. For Job said, "It may be that my sons have …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “His possessions also were seven thousand sheep, three thousand camels, ______ hundred yoke of oxen, five hundred she-donkeys, and a very great household; so that this …” (1:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: five. The cited words are “His possessions also were seven thousand sheep, three thousand camels, five hundred yoke of oxen, five hundred she-donkeys, and a very great household; so that this …”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… each one on his birthday; and they sent and called for their ______ sisters to eat and to drink with them.”? (1:4, WEB) Easy
A) five B) ninth C) forty D) three
Answer: D. three. Job 1:4 uses three: “… each one on his birthday; and they sent and called for their three sisters to eat and to drink with them.”
Job 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… forth your hand now, and touch his bone and his ______, and he will ______ you to your face."” (2:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: flesh; renounce. The wording is “… forth your hand now, and touch his bone and his flesh, and he will renounce you to your face."” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Yahweh said to Satan, "Behold, he is in your hand. Only spare his life."” (2:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Yahweh said to Satan, "Behold, he is in your hand. Only spare his life."”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… Yahweh, and struck Job with painful sores from the sole of his ______ to his head.”? (2:7, WEB) Medium
A) teeth B) heart C) feet D) foot
Answer: D. foot. The passage specifies foot: “… Yahweh, and struck Job with painful sores from the sole of his foot to his head.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… God, and shall we not receive evil?" In all this Job didn't ______ with his lips.” (2:10); “… came from his own place: Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite, and ______ the Naamathite, and they made an appointment together to come to sympathize with …” (2:11, WEB) Hard
A) sin; Zophar B) wickedness; Zophar C) sin; Shem D) Zophar; sin
Answer: A. sin; Zophar. Verse 10 reads “… God, and shall we not receive evil?" In all this Job didn't sin with his lips.”; verse 11 reads “… came from his own place: Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite, and Zophar the Naamathite, and they made an appointment together to come to sympathize with …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, job's wife said, Curse God and ______. (2:9) Easy
Answer: die. Job 2:9 records: “Then his wife said to him, "Do you still maintain your integrity? Renounce God, and die."”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… each tore his robe, and sprinkled dust on their heads toward the ______.” (2:12); “So they sat down with him on the ground seven days and ______ nights, and none spoke a word to him, for they saw that his …” (2:13, WEB) Hard
Answer: sky; seven. The first passage reads “… each tore his robe, and sprinkled dust on their heads toward the sky.”; the second reads “So they sat down with him on the ground seven days and seven nights, and none spoke a word to him, for they saw that his …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Yahweh said to Satan, "Have you considered my servant ______? For there is none like him in the earth, a blameless and an upright man, one …” (2:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Job. The cited words are “Yahweh said to Satan, "Have you considered my servant Job? For there is none like him in the earth, a blameless and an upright man, one …”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… skin. Yes, all that a man has will he give for his ______.”? (2:4, WEB) Easy
A) worship B) strength C) deliverance D) life
Answer: D. life. Job 2:4 uses life: “… skin. Yes, all that a man has will he give for his life.”
Job 3
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “"Why is light given to him who is in misery, ______ to the bitter in soul,” (3:20); “… my sighing comes before I eat, My groanings are poured out like ______.” (3:24, WEB) Hard
Answer: Life; water. The first passage reads “"Why is light given to him who is in misery, Life to the bitter in soul,”; the second reads “… my sighing comes before I eat, My groanings are poured out like water.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the days of the year. Let it not come into the ______ of the ______.” (3:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: number; months. The wording is “… the days of the year. Let it not come into the number of the months.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Or with princes who had gold, Who filled their houses with ______:” (3:15); “… prisoners are at ease together. They don't hear the voice of the ______.” (3:18, WEB) Hard
A) taskmaster; silver B) silver; dark C) stone; taskmaster D) silver; taskmaster
Answer: D. silver; taskmaster. Verse 15 reads “Or with princes who had gold, Who filled their houses with silver:”; verse 18 reads “… prisoners are at ease together. They don't hear the voice of the taskmaster.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Let ______ and the shadow of death claim it for their own. Let a cloud dwell on it. Let all that makes black the day terrify …”? (3:5, WEB) Easy
A) mercy B) glory C) water D) darkness
Answer: D. darkness. Job 3:5 uses darkness: “Let darkness and the shadow of death claim it for their own. Let a cloud dwell on it. Let all that makes black the day terrify …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Because it didn't shut up the ______ of my mother's womb, Nor did it hide trouble from my eyes.”? (3:10, WEB) Medium
A) doors B) mother's C) comes D) places
Answer: A. doors. The passage specifies doors: “Because it didn't shut up the doors of my mother's womb, Nor did it hide trouble from my eyes.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “With kings and counselors of the earth, Who built up waste places for themselves;” (3:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “With kings and counselors of the earth, Who built up waste places for themselves;”
Q7. True or false: Job cursed the day of his birth. (3:1) Easy
Answer: True. Job 3:1 records: “After this Job opened his mouth, and cursed the day of his birth.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… which I was born, The night which said, 'There is a man-child ______.'” (3:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: conceived. The cited words are “… which I was born, The night which said, 'There is a man-child conceived.'”
Job 4
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Multiple choice: Why should Eliphaz's claim that the innocent do not perish be read as part of a debate? (4:7-8; 42:7) Hard
A) Eliphaz has already heard God's final response. B) It is Eliphaz's explanation of suffering, and God later rebukes the friends' speech. C) Job's friends never make claims about suffering. D) Every statement by every speaker is God's verdict.
Answer: B. It is Eliphaz's explanation of suffering, and God later rebukes the friends' speech.. A character's words are genuinely recorded without every claim becoming the book's final teaching. The course's context rule is especially important in Job's dialogues.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “In ______ from the visions of the night, When deep sleep falls on men,” (4:13); “Then a ______ passed before my face; The hair of my flesh stood up.” (4:15, WEB) Hard
A) visions; spirit B) thoughts; plow C) thoughts; spirit D) spirit; thoughts
Answer: C. thoughts; spirit. Verse 13 reads “In thoughts from the visions of the night, When deep sleep falls on men,”; verse 15 reads “Then a spirit passed before my face; The hair of my flesh stood up.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Your words have supported him who was falling, You have made ______ the feeble knees.”? (4:4, WEB) Easy
A) visions B) firm C) discern D) angels
Answer: B. firm. Job 4:4 uses firm: “Your words have supported him who was falling, You have made firm the feeble knees.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Isn't your ______ your confidence, The integrity of your ways your ______?” (4:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: piety; hope. The wording is “Isn't your piety your confidence, The integrity of your ways your hope?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, eliphaz said that the innocent do not ______. (4:7) Easy
Answer: perish. Job 4:7 records: “"Remember, now, whoever perished, being innocent? Or where were the upright cut off?”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “"If someone ventures to talk with you, will you be ______? But who can withhold himself from speaking?” (4:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: grieved. The cited words are “"If someone ventures to talk with you, will you be grieved? But who can withhold himself from speaking?”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… roaring of the lion, and the voice of the fierce lion, The lips of the young lions, are broken.” (4:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… roaring of the lion, and the voice of the fierce lion, The teeth of the young lions, are broken.” It says teeth, not lips.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “According to what I have seen, those who plow ______, And sow trouble, Reap the same.”? (4:8, WEB) Medium
A) deceit B) wickedness C) transgression D) iniquity
Answer: D. iniquity. The passage specifies iniquity: “According to what I have seen, those who plow iniquity, And sow trouble, Reap the same.”
Job 5
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For resentment kills the foolish man, And jealousy kills the ______.” (5:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: simple. The cited words are “For resentment kills the foolish man, And jealousy kills the simple.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “So the ______ has hope, And injustice shuts her mouth.” (5:16); “He will ______ you in six troubles; Yes, in seven there shall no evil touch you.” (5:19, WEB) Hard
A) poor; hungry B) deliver; poor C) unclean; deliver D) poor; deliver
Answer: D. poor; deliver. Verse 16 reads “So the poor has hope, And injustice shuts her mouth.”; verse 19 reads “He will deliver you in six troubles; Yes, in seven there shall no evil touch you.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Who gives ______ on the earth, And sends waters on the fields;”? (5:10, WEB) Medium
A) cause B) rain C) shock D) peace
Answer: B. rain. The passage specifies rain: “Who gives rain on the earth, And sends waters on the fields;”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “But man is born to trouble, As the ______ ______ upward.” (5:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: sparks; fly. The wording is “But man is born to trouble, As the sparks fly upward.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… hungry eats up, And take it even out of the thorns; The ______ gapes for their substance.”? (5:5, WEB) Easy
A) snare B) seven C) enterprise D) miss
Answer: A. snare. Job 5:5 uses snare: “… hungry eats up, And take it even out of the thorns; The snare gapes for their substance.”
Q6. True or false: Eliphaz told Job to seek God. (5:8) Easy
Answer: True. Job 5:8 records: “"But as for me, I would seek God, To God would I commit my cause;”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… be hidden from the scourge of the tongue, Neither shall you be ______ of destruction when it comes.” (5:21); “You shall know that your tent is in ______. You shall visit your fold, and shall miss nothing.” (5:24, WEB) Hard
Answer: afraid; peace. The first passage reads “… be hidden from the scourge of the tongue, Neither shall you be afraid of destruction when it comes.”; the second reads “You shall know that your tent is in peace. You shall visit your fold, and shall miss nothing.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “He takes the unclean in their own craftiness; The counsel of the cunning is carried headlong.” (5:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “He takes the wise in their own craftiness; The counsel of the cunning is carried headlong.” It says wise, not unclean.
Job 6
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, job said his friends were like a ______ brook. (6:15) Easy
Answer: deceitful. Job 6:15 records: “My brothers have dealt deceitfully as a brook, As the channel of brooks that pass away;”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Is my strength the strength of stones? Or is my flesh of ______?”? (6:12, WEB) Medium
A) brass B) wood C) stones D) iron
Answer: A. brass. The passage specifies brass: “Is my strength the strength of stones? Or is my flesh of brass?”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… should be shown from his friend; Even to him who forsakes the fear of the Almighty.” (6:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… should be shown from his friend; Even to him who forsakes the fear of the Almighty.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For the arrows of the Almighty are within me, My ______ drinks up their poison. The terrors of God set themselves in array against me.” (6:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: spirit. The cited words are “For the arrows of the Almighty are within me, My spirit drinks up their poison. The terrors of God set themselves in array against me.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Can that which has no ______ be eaten without salt? Or is there any taste in the white of an egg?”? (6:6, WEB) Easy
A) denied B) flavor C) stones D) kindness
Answer: B. flavor. Job 6:6 uses flavor: “Can that which has no flavor be eaten without salt? Or is there any taste in the white of an egg?”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “"Teach me, and I will hold my ______; Cause me to understand wherein I have erred.” (6:24); “Do you intend to reprove words, ______ that the speeches of one who is desperate are as wind?” (6:26, WEB) Hard
Answer: peace; Seeing. The first passage reads “"Teach me, and I will hold my peace; Cause me to understand wherein I have erred.”; the second reads “Do you intend to reprove words, Seeing that the speeches of one who is desperate are as wind?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… travel beside them turn aside; They go up into the waste, and ______.” (6:18); “The caravans of Tema looked, The companies of ______ waited for them.” (6:19, WEB) Hard
A) mischievous; Sheba B) perish; Please C) Sheba; perish D) perish; Sheba
Answer: D. perish; Sheba. Verse 18 reads “… travel beside them turn aside; They go up into the waste, and perish.”; verse 19 reads “The caravans of Tema looked, The companies of Sheba waited for them.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… my consolation, Yes, let me ______ in pain that doesn't spare, That I have not denied the words of the ______ One.” (6:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: exult; Holy. The wording is “… my consolation, Yes, let me exult in pain that doesn't spare, That I have not denied the words of the Holy One.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Job 7
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… with worms and clods of dust. My skin closes up, and ______ out ______.” (7:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: breaks; afresh. The wording is “… with worms and clods of dust. My skin closes up, and breaks out afresh.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “When I say, 'My bed shall ______ me, My couch shall ease my complaint;'” (7:13); “… to live forever. Leave me alone; for my days are but a ______.” (7:16, WEB) Hard
A) comfort; breath B) comfort; desires C) breath; comfort D) burden; breath
Answer: A. comfort; breath. Verse 13 reads “When I say, 'My bed shall comfort me, My couch shall ease my complaint;'”; verse 16 reads “… to live forever. Leave me alone; for my days are but a breath.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… 'When shall I arise, and the night be gone?' I toss and ______ until the dawning of the day.”? (7:4, WEB) Easy
A) turn B) like C) looks D) spirit
Answer: A. turn. Job 7:4 uses turn: “… 'When shall I arise, and the night be gone?' I toss and turn until the dawning of the day.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “As a ______ who earnestly desires the shadow, As a hireling who looks for his wages,” (7:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: servant. The cited words are “As a servant who earnestly desires the shadow, As a hireling who looks for his wages,”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “So that my soul chooses strangling, ______ rather than my bones.” (7:15); “… away from me, Nor leave me alone until I swallow down my ______?” (7:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: Death; spittle. The first passage reads “So that my soul chooses strangling, Death rather than my bones.”; the second reads “… away from me, Nor leave me alone until I swallow down my spittle?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. True or false: Job asked why God set him as a target. (7:20) Easy
Answer: True. Job 7:20 records: “If I have sinned, what do I do to you, you watcher of men? Why have you set me as a mark for you, So that I am a burden to myself?”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… will not keep silent. I will speak in the anguish of my skin. I will complain in the bitterness of my soul.” (7:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… will not keep silent. I will speak in the anguish of my spirit. I will complain in the bitterness of my soul.” It says spirit, not skin.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… cloud is consumed and vanishes away, So he who goes down to ______ shall come up no more.”? (7:9, WEB) Medium
A) complain B) Sheol C) hope D) desires
Answer: B. Sheol. The passage specifies Sheol: “… cloud is consumed and vanishes away, So he who goes down to Sheol shall come up no more.”
Job 8
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… were pure and upright, Surely now he would ______ for you, And make the habitation of your ______ prosperous.” (8:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: awaken; righteousness. The wording is “… were pure and upright, Surely now he would awaken for you, And make the habitation of your righteousness prosperous.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… of all who forget God. The hope of the godless man shall ______,” (8:13); “His roots are wrapped around the ______ pile, He sees the place of stones.” (8:17, WEB) Hard
A) perish; days B) rock; perish C) lean; rock D) perish; rock
Answer: D. perish; rock. Verse 13 reads “… of all who forget God. The hope of the godless man shall perish,”; verse 17 reads “His roots are wrapped around the rock pile, He sees the place of stones.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… speak these things? Shall the words of your mouth be a mighty ______?” (8:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: wind. The cited words are “… speak these things? Shall the words of your mouth be a mighty wind?”
Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, bildad said God does not pervert ______. (8:3) Easy
Answer: justice. Job 8:3 records: “Does God pervert justice? Or does the Almighty pervert righteousness?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Behold, this is the ______ of his way: Out of the earth shall others spring.” (8:19); “"Behold, God will not cast away a ______ man, Neither will he uphold the evil-doers.” (8:20, WEB) Hard
Answer: joy; blameless. The first passage reads “Behold, this is the joy of his way: Out of the earth shall others spring.”; the second reads “"Behold, God will not cast away a blameless man, Neither will he uphold the evil-doers.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “If your children have sinned against him, He has ______ them into the hand of their disobedience;”? (8:4, WEB) Easy
A) wrapped B) sinned C) reed D) delivered
Answer: D. delivered. Job 8:4 uses delivered: “If your children have sinned against him, He has delivered them into the hand of their disobedience;”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “"Can the papyrus grow up without mire? Can the rushes grow without water?” (8:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “"Can the papyrus grow up without mire? Can the rushes grow without water?”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “"Please inquire of past ______, Find out about the learning of their fathers.”? (8:8, WEB) Medium
A) generations B) godless C) righteousness D) prosperous
Answer: A. generations. The passage specifies generations: “"Please inquire of past generations, Find out about the learning of their fathers.”
Job 9
These chapters record a debate. Identify the speaker before treating a statement as the book's conclusion; compare God's rebuke of the friends in Job 42:7-9.
Q1. True or false: Job said there was no umpire between him and God. (9:33) Easy
Answer: True. Job 9:33 records: “There is no umpire between us, That might lay his hand on us both.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… to contend with him, He can't answer him one time in a ______.” (9:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: thousand. The cited words are “… to contend with him, He can't answer him one time in a thousand.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For he breaks me with a tempest, Multiplies my wounds without cause.” (9:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “For he breaks me with a tempest, Multiplies my wounds without cause.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Though I am ______, my own mouth shall condemn me. Though I am blameless, it shall prove me perverse.” (9:20); “… into the hand of the wicked. He covers the faces of the ______ of it. If not he, then who is it?” (9:24, WEB) Hard
A) holy; judges B) righteous; daughters C) judges; righteous D) righteous; judges
Answer: D. righteous; judges. Verse 20 reads “Though I am righteous, my own mouth shall condemn me. Though I am blameless, it shall prove me perverse.”; verse 24 reads “… into the hand of the wicked. He covers the faces of the judges of it. If not he, then who is it?”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Who ______ the sun, and it doesn't rise, And seals up the stars;”? (9:7, WEB) Easy
A) passes B) helpers C) commands D) Pleiades
Answer: C. commands. Job 9:7 uses commands: “Who commands the sun, and it doesn't rise, And seals up the stars;”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “If I say, 'I will forget my ______, I will put off my sad face, and cheer up;'” (9:27); “Yet you will plunge me in the ditch. My own ______ shall abhor me.” (9:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: complaint; clothes. The first passage reads “If I say, 'I will forget my complaint, I will put off my sad face, and cheer up;'”; the second reads “Yet you will plunge me in the ditch. My own clothes shall abhor me.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Who does great things past ______ out, Yes, ______ things without number.” (9:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: finding; marvelous. The wording is “Who does great things past finding out, Yes, marvelous things without number.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “"God will not ______ his anger; The helpers of Rahab stoop under him.”? (9:13, WEB) Medium
A) withdraw B) place C) cheer D) stretches
Answer: A. withdraw. The passage specifies withdraw: “"God will not withdraw his anger; The helpers of Rahab stoop under him.”
Job 10

Answer: walled; darkness. The wording is “He has walled up my way so that I can't pass, And has set darkness in my paths.” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: True. Job 19:25 records: “But as for me, I know that my Redeemer lives. In the end, he will stand upon the earth.”

Answer: B. gold; equal. Verse 16 reads “It can't be valued with the gold of Ophir, With the precious onyx, or the sapphire.”; verse 19 reads “The topaz of Ethiopia shall not equal it, Neither shall it be valued with pure gold.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… eyes of all living, And kept close from the birds of the ______.” (28:21); “… the force of the wind; Yes, he measures out the waters by ______.” (28:25, WEB) Hard
Answer: sky; measure. The first passage reads “… eyes of all living, And kept close from the birds of the sky.”; the second reads “… the force of the wind; Yes, he measures out the waters by measure.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… shall tell him; For we can't make our case by reason of ______.” (37:19); “Now men don't see the ______ which is bright in the skies, But the wind passes, and clears them.” (37:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: darkness; light. The first passage reads “… shall tell him; For we can't make our case by reason of darkness.”; the second reads “Now men don't see the light which is bright in the skies, But the wind passes, and clears them.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… they may do whatever he commands them On the surface of the habitable world,” (37:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… they may do whatever he commands them On the surface of the habitable world,”
Job 38
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ yourself like a man, For I will question you, then you answer me!” (38:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Brace. The cited words are “Brace yourself like a man, For I will question you, then you answer me!”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “"What is the way to the dwelling of light? As for fear, where is the place of it,” (38:19, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “"What is the way to the dwelling of light? As for darkness, where is the place of it,” It says darkness, not fear.
Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, god answered Job out of the ______. (38:1) Easy
Answer: whirlwind. Job 38:1 records: “Then Yahweh answered Job out of the whirlwind,”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Can you lead forth the constellations in their ______? Or can you guide the Bear with her cubs?” (38:32); “Who has put wisdom in the inward parts? Or who has given ______ to the mind?” (38:36, WEB) Hard
Answer: season; understanding. The first passage reads “Can you lead forth the constellations in their season? Or can you guide the Bear with her cubs?”; the second reads “Who has put wisdom in the inward parts? Or who has given understanding to the mind?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… morning stars sang together, And all the sons of God shouted for ______?”? (38:7, WEB) Easy
A) peace B) joy C) wrath D) truth
Answer: B. joy. Job 38:7 uses joy: “… morning stars sang together, And all the sons of God shouted for joy?”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “By what way is the ______ distributed, Or the east wind scattered on the earth?” (38:24); “To satisfy the waste and desolate ground, To cause the ______ grass to spring forth?” (38:27, WEB) Hard
A) lightning; sons B) lightning; tender C) morning; tender D) tender; lightning
Answer: B. lightning; tender. Verse 24 reads “By what way is the lightning distributed, Or the east wind scattered on the earth?”; verse 27 reads “To satisfy the waste and desolate ground, To cause the tender grass to spring forth?”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “"Have you ______ the ______ in your days, And caused the dawn to know its place;” (38:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: commanded; morning. The wording is “"Have you commanded the morning in your days, And caused the dawn to know its place;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “From the wicked, their ______ is withheld, The high arm is broken.”? (38:15, WEB) Medium
A) mass B) light C) broke D) water
Answer: B. light. The passage specifies light: “From the wicked, their light is withheld, The high arm is broken.”
Job 39
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Can you number the months that they ______? Or do you know the time when they give birth?” (39:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: fulfill. The cited words are “Can you number the months that they fulfill? Or do you know the time when they give birth?”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “"Will the ______ ox be content to serve you? Or will he ______ by your feeding trough?” (39:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: wild; stay. The wording is “"Will the wild ox be content to serve you? Or will he stay by your feeding trough?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “"Who has set the wild ______ free? Or who has loosened the bonds of the swift donkey,”? (39:5, WEB) Easy
A) eagles B) lamb C) dove D) donkey
Answer: D. donkey. Job 39:5 uses donkey: “"Who has set the wild donkey free? Or who has loosened the bonds of the swift donkey,”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He eats up the ground with ______ and rage, Neither does he stand still at the sound of the trumpet.” (39:24); “"Is it by your ______ that the hawk soars, And stretches her wings toward the south?” (39:26, WEB) Hard
Answer: fierceness; wisdom. The first passage reads “He eats up the ground with fierceness and rage, Neither does he stand still at the sound of the trumpet.”; the second reads “"Is it by your wisdom that the hawk soars, And stretches her wings toward the south?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… God has deprived her of wisdom, Neither has he imparted to her ______.” (39:17); “He paws in the valley, and rejoices in his strength: He ______ out to meet the armed men.” (39:21, WEB) Hard
A) understanding; goes B) truth; goes C) understanding; lifts D) goes; understanding
Answer: A. understanding; goes. Verse 17 reads “… God has deprived her of wisdom, Neither has he imparted to her understanding.”; verse 21 reads “He paws in the valley, and rejoices in his strength: He goes out to meet the armed men.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… in him, that he will bring home your seed, And gather the ______ of your threshing floor?”? (39:12, WEB) Medium
A) wheat B) meat C) wine D) grain
Answer: D. grain. The passage specifies grain: “… in him, that he will bring home your seed, And gather the grain of your threshing floor?”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… the ostrich wave proudly; But are they the feathers and plumage of peace?” (39:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… the ostrich wave proudly; But are they the feathers and plumage of love?” It says love, not peace.
Q8. True or false: God asked Job about the wild goat giving birth on the rocks. (39:1) Easy
Answer: True. Job 39:1 records: “"Do you know the time when the mountain goats give birth? Do you watch when the doe bears fawns?”
Job 40
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… argues contend with the Almighty? He who argues with God, let him ______ it."” (40:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: answer. The cited words are “… argues contend with the Almighty? He who argues with God, let him answer it."”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Will you even annul my ______? Will you condemn me, that you may be ______?” (40:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: judgment; justified. The wording is “Will you even annul my judgment? Will you condemn me, that you may be justified?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Look on whirlwind who is proud, and humble him. Crush the wicked in their place.” (40:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Look on everyone who is proud, and humble him. Crush the wicked in their place.” It says everyone, not whirlwind.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “"Behold, I am of small ______. What shall I answer you? I lay my hand on my mouth.”? (40:4, WEB) Easy
A) knit B) animals C) surround D) account
Answer: D. account. Job 40:4 uses account: “"Behold, I am of small account. What shall I answer you? I lay my hand on my mouth.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Hide them in the dust together. Bind their ______ in the hidden place.” (40:13); “He moves his tail like a cedar: The sinews of his ______ are knit together.” (40:17, WEB) Hard
A) faces; thighs B) faces; willows C) thighs; faces D) argues; thighs
Answer: A. faces; thighs. Verse 13 reads “Hide them in the dust together. Bind their faces in the hidden place.”; verse 17 reads “He moves his tail like a cedar: The sinews of his thighs are knit together.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, god challenged Job to dress for battle like a ______. (40:7) Easy
Answer: man. Job 40:7 records: “"Now brace yourself like a man. I will question you, and you will answer me.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “"Now deck yourself with excellency and dignity. Array yourself with honor and ______.”? (40:10, WEB) Medium
A) condemn B) account C) bars D) majesty
Answer: D. majesty. The passage specifies majesty: “"Now deck yourself with excellency and dignity. Array yourself with honor and majesty.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “His bones are like tubes of ______. His limbs are like bars of iron.” (40:18); “… if a river overflows, he doesn't tremble. He is confident, though the ______ swells even to his mouth.” (40:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: brass; Jordan. The first passage reads “His bones are like tubes of brass. His limbs are like bars of iron.”; the second reads “… if a river overflows, he doesn't tremble. He is confident, though the Jordan swells even to his mouth.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Job 41
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “If one lay at him with the sword, it ______ avail; Nor the spear, the dart, nor the pointed shaft.” (41:26); “His undersides are like sharp ______, Leaving a trail in the mud like a threshing sledge.” (41:30, WEB) Hard
Answer: can't; potsherds. The first passage reads “If one lay at him with the sword, it can't avail; Nor the spear, the dart, nor the pointed shaft.”; the second reads “His undersides are like sharp potsherds, Leaving a trail in the mud like a threshing sledge.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “None is so fierce that he ______ ______ him up. Who then is he who can stand before me?” (41:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: dare; stir. The wording is “None is so fierce that he dare stir him up. Who then is he who can stand before me?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. True or false: God described Leviathan, a powerful creature of the sea. (41:1) Easy
Answer: True. Job 41:1 records: “"Can you draw out Leviathan with a fishhook? Or press down his tongue with a cord?”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Can you put a rope into his ______? Or pierce his jaw through with a hook?” (41:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: nose. The cited words are “Can you put a rope into his nose? Or pierce his jaw through with a hook?”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Who can strip off his ______ garment? Who shall come within his jaws?”? (41:13, WEB) Medium
A) sword B) stubble C) outer D) shaft
Answer: C. outer. The passage specifies outer: “Who can strip off his outer garment? Who shall come within his jaws?”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… joined one to another; They stick together, so that they can't be moved apart.” (41:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… joined one to another; They stick together, so that they can't be pulled apart.” It says pulled, not moved.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Can you fill his skin with ______ irons, Or his head with fish-spears?”? (41:7, WEB) Easy
A) moved B) pulled C) counted D) barbed
Answer: D. barbed. Job 41:7 uses barbed: “Can you fill his skin with barbed irons, Or his head with fish-spears?”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… his nostrils a smoke goes, As of a boiling pot over a ______ of reeds.” (41:20); “The flakes of his ______ are joined together. They are firm on him. They can't be moved.” (41:23, WEB) Hard
A) spear; flesh B) flesh; fire C) fire; flesh D) fire; mouth
Answer: C. fire; flesh. Verse 20 reads “… his nostrils a smoke goes, As of a boiling pot over a fire of reeds.”; verse 23 reads “The flakes of his flesh are joined together. They are firm on him. They can't be moved.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Job 42
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You asked, 'Who is this who hides counsel without ______?' Therefore I have uttered that which I did not understand, Things too wonderful for me, which …” (42:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: knowledge. The cited words are “You asked, 'Who is this who hides counsel without knowledge?' Therefore I have uttered that which I did not understand, Things too wonderful for me, which …”
Q2. Multiple choice: What does God's rebuke of the friends add to the ending? (42:7-9) Hard
A) Their accusations are all confirmed. B) Their confident speeches must not be accepted uncritically as right speech about God. C) Job is forbidden to pray for anyone. D) The book says theology has no effect on conduct.
Answer: B. Their confident speeches must not be accepted uncritically as right speech about God.. God calls for them to bring offerings and receive Job's prayer. The ending corrects their treatment of Job rather than endorsing every accusation they made.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “It was so, that after Yahweh had spoken these words to ______, Yahweh said to Eliphaz the Temanite, "My wrath is kindled against you, and against …”? (42:7, WEB) Easy
A) Nathan B) Job C) Amos D) David
Answer: B. Job. Job 42:7 uses Job: “It was so, that after Yahweh had spoken these words to Job, Yahweh said to Eliphaz the Temanite, "My wrath is kindled against you, and against …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, god restored Job's fortunes and gave him twice as much as he had ______. (42:10) Easy
Answer: before. Job 42:10 records: “Yahweh turned the captivity of Job, when he prayed for his friends. Yahweh gave Job twice as much as he had before.”
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “So Joshua the Temanite and Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite went, and did what Yahweh commanded them, and Yahweh accepted Job.” (42:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “So Eliphaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite went, and did what Yahweh commanded them, and Yahweh accepted Job.” It says Eliphaz, not Joshua.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… servant Job, and offer up for yourselves a burnt offering; and my ______ Job shall pray for you, for I will accept him, that I not …”? (42:8, WEB) Medium
A) priests B) prophets C) servant D) shepherd
Answer: C. servant. The passage specifies servant: “… servant Job, and offer up for yourselves a burnt offering; and my servant Job shall pray for you, for I will accept him, that I not …”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… also gave him a piece of money, and everyone a ring of ______.” (42:11); “… of Job more than his beginning. He had fourteen thousand sheep, six ______ camels, one thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand female donkeys.” (42:12, WEB) Hard
A) gold; thousand B) gold; ten C) clay; thousand D) thousand; gold
Answer: A. gold; thousand. Verse 11 reads “… also gave him a piece of money, and everyone a ring of gold.”; verse 12 reads “… of Job more than his beginning. He had fourteen thousand sheep, six thousand camels, one thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand female donkeys.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… heard of you by the hearing of the ear, But now my ______ ______ you.” (42:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: eye; sees. The wording is “… heard of you by the hearing of the ear, But now my eye sees you.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Psalms
Psalms 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He will be like a tree planted by the streams of ______, That brings forth its fruit in its season, Whose leaf also does not wither. …”? (1:3, WEB) Easy
A) corn B) water C) pomegranates D) wheat
Answer: B. water. Psalms 1:3 uses water: “He will be like a tree planted by the streams of water, That brings forth its fruit in its season, Whose leaf also does not wither. …”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For Yahweh knows the way of the foolish, But the way of the wicked shall perish.” (1:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “For Yahweh knows the way of the righteous, But the way of the wicked shall perish.” It says righteous, not foolish.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ is the man who doesn't walk in the counsel of the wicked, Nor stand in the way of sinners, Nor sit in the seat of …” (1:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Blessed. The cited words are “Blessed is the man who doesn't walk in the counsel of the wicked, Nor stand in the way of sinners, Nor sit in the seat of …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Therefore the wicked shall not ______ in the judgment, Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.” (1:5); “… the way of the righteous, But the way of the wicked shall ______.” (1:6, WEB) Hard
Answer: stand; perish. The first passage reads “Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment, Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.”; the second reads “… the way of the righteous, But the way of the wicked shall perish.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. True or false: The blessed man delights in the law of the LORD and meditates on it. (1:2) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 1:2 records: “But his delight is in Yahweh's law; On his law he meditates day and night.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “He will be like a tree ______ by the streams of water, That brings forth its fruit in its season, Whose leaf also does not wither. …” (1:3); “Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment, Nor ______ in the congregation of the righteous.” (1:5, WEB) Hard
A) salvation; sinners B) sinners; planted C) planted; knows D) planted; sinners
Answer: D. planted; sinners. Verse 3 reads “He will be like a tree planted by the streams of water, That brings forth its fruit in its season, Whose leaf also does not wither. …”; verse 5 reads “Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment, Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The ______ are not so, But are like the ______ which the wind drives away.” (1:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: wicked; chaff. The wording is “The wicked are not so, But are like the chaff which the wind drives away.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the ______, Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.”? (1:5, WEB) Medium
A) teaching B) judgment C) word D) promises
Answer: B. judgment. The passage specifies judgment: “Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment, Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.”
Psalms 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The ______ of the earth take a stand, And the rulers take counsel together, Against Yahweh, and against his anointed, saying,” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: kings. The cited words are “The kings of the earth take a stand, And the rulers take counsel together, Against Yahweh, and against his anointed, saying,”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “He who ______ in the heavens will laugh. The Lord will have them in ______.” (2:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: sits; derision. The wording is “He who sits in the heavens will laugh. The Lord will have them in derision.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Ask of me, and I will give the pieces for your inheritance, The uttermost parts of the earth for your possession.” (2:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Ask of me, and I will give the nations for your inheritance, The uttermost parts of the earth for your possession.” It says nations, not pieces.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “You shall break them with a ______ of iron. You shall dash them in pieces like a potter's vessel."” (2:9); “Now therefore be ______, you kings. Be instructed, you judges of the earth.” (2:10, WEB) Hard
A) rod; wise B) vain; wise C) rod; foolish D) wise; rod
Answer: A. rod; wise. Verse 9 reads “You shall break them with a rod of iron. You shall dash them in pieces like a potter's vessel."”; verse 10 reads “Now therefore be wise, you kings. Be instructed, you judges of the earth.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, psalm 2 calls kings to serve the LORD and ______ His Son. (2:11-12) Easy
Answer: kiss. Psalms 2:12 records: “Kiss the son, lest he be angry, and you perish in the way, For his wrath will soon be kindled. Blessed are all those who take refuge in him.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “I will tell of the ______. Yahweh said to me, "You are my son. Today I have become your father.” (2:7); “Then he will speak to them in his ______, And terrify them in his wrath:” (2:5, WEB) Hard
Answer: decree; anger. The first passage reads “I will tell of the decree. Yahweh said to me, "You are my son. Today I have become your father.”; the second reads “Then he will speak to them in his anger, And terrify them in his wrath:”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “"Let's ______ their bonds apart, And cast away their cords from us."”? (2:3, WEB) Easy
A) remember B) break C) forsake D) love
Answer: B. break. Psalms 2:3 uses break: “"Let's break their bonds apart, And cast away their cords from us."”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “"Yet I have set my king on my holy hill of ______."”? (2:6, WEB) Medium
A) Damascus B) Canaan C) Jericho D) Zion
Answer: D. Zion. The passage specifies Zion: “"Yet I have set my king on my holy hill of Zion."”
Psalms 3
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Many there are who say of my ______, "There is no ______ for him in God." Selah.” (3:2, WEB) Medium
Answer: soul; help. The wording is “Many there are who say of my soul, "There is no help for him in God." Selah.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “I will not be afraid of ______ of thousands of people Who have set themselves against me on every side.” (3:6); “______ belongs to Yahweh. Your blessing be on your people. Selah.” (3:8, WEB) Hard
A) Salvation; tens B) tens; Salvation C) belongs; Salvation D) tens; inheritance
Answer: B. tens; Salvation. Verse 6 reads “I will not be afraid of tens of thousands of people Who have set themselves against me on every side.”; verse 8 reads “Salvation belongs to Yahweh. Your blessing be on your people. Selah.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “I laid myself down and slept. I law; for Yahweh sustains me.” (3:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “I laid myself down and slept. I awakened; for Yahweh sustains me.” It says awakened, not law.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… to Yahweh with my voice, And he answers me out of his ______ hill. Selah.”? (3:4, WEB) Easy
A) wise B) holy C) righteous D) faithful
Answer: B. holy. Psalms 3:4 uses holy: “… to Yahweh with my voice, And he answers me out of his holy hill. Selah.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “But you, Yahweh, are a shield around me, My ______, and the one who lifts up my head.” (3:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: glory. The cited words are “But you, Yahweh, are a shield around me, My glory, and the one who lifts up my head.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Arise, Yahweh! Save me, my God! For you have ______ all of my enemies on the cheek bone. You have broken the teeth of the wicked.”? (3:7, WEB) Medium
A) Arise B) teeth C) struck D) soul
Answer: C. struck. The passage specifies struck: “Arise, Yahweh! Save me, my God! For you have struck all of my enemies on the cheek bone. You have broken the teeth of the wicked.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… have struck all of my enemies on the cheek bone. You have ______ the teeth of the wicked.” (3:7); “Salvation ______ to Yahweh. Your blessing be on your people. Selah.” (3:8, WEB) Hard
Answer: broken; belongs. The first passage reads “… have struck all of my enemies on the cheek bone. You have broken the teeth of the wicked.”; the second reads “Salvation belongs to Yahweh. Your blessing be on your people. Selah.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: David wrote Psalm 3 while fleeing from his son Absalom. (3:1) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 3:1 records: “Yahweh, how my adversaries have increased! Many are those who rise up against me.”
Psalms 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You sons of men, how long shall my ______ be turned into dishonor? Will you love vanity, and seek after falsehood? Selah.” (4:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: glory. The cited words are “You sons of men, how long shall my glory be turned into dishonor? Will you love vanity, and seek after falsehood? Selah.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Answer me when I call, God of my ______. Give me relief from my distress. Have mercy on me, and hear my prayer.”? (4:1, WEB) Easy
A) understanding B) glory C) righteousness D) wrath
Answer: C. righteousness. Psalms 4:1 uses righteousness: “Answer me when I call, God of my righteousness. Give me relief from my distress. Have mercy on me, and hear my prayer.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the psalmist said, In peace I will both lie down and ______. (4:8) Easy
Answer: sleep. Psalms 4:8 records: “In peace I will both lay myself down and sleep, For you, Yahweh alone, make me live in safety.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Many say, "Who will show us any good?" Yahweh, let the ______ of your face shine on us.” (4:6); “But know that Yahweh has set ______ for himself him who is godly: Yahweh will hear when I call to him.” (4:3, WEB) Hard
A) grain; apart B) apart; light C) light; apart D) light; turned
Answer: C. light; apart. Verse 6 reads “Many say, "Who will show us any good?" Yahweh, let the light of your face shine on us.”; verse 3 reads “But know that Yahweh has set apart for himself him who is godly: Yahweh will hear when I call to him.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “You have put gladness in my heart, More than when their pomegranates and their new wine are increased.” (4:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “You have put gladness in my heart, More than when their grain and their new wine are increased.” It says grain, not pomegranates.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Offer the ______ of righteousness. Put your trust in Yahweh.”? (4:5, WEB) Medium
A) righteousness B) distress C) sons D) sacrifices
Answer: D. sacrifices. The passage specifies sacrifices: “Offer the sacrifices of righteousness. Put your trust in Yahweh.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Stand in awe, and don't ______. Search your own ______ on your bed, and be still. Selah.” (4:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: sin; heart. The wording is “Stand in awe, and don't sin. Search your own heart on your bed, and be still. Selah.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… gladness in my heart, More than when their grain and their new ______ are increased.” (4:7); “In ______ I will both lay myself down and sleep, For you, Yahweh alone, make me live in safety.” (4:8, WEB) Hard
Answer: wine; peace. The first passage reads “… gladness in my heart, More than when their grain and their new wine are increased.”; the second reads “In peace I will both lay myself down and sleep, For you, Yahweh alone, make me live in safety.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Psalms 5
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Listen to the voice of my cry, my ______ and my God; For to you do I pray.” (5:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: King. The cited words are “Listen to the voice of my cry, my King and my God; For to you do I pray.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… by their own counsels; Thrust them out in the multitude of their ______, For they have rebelled against you.” (5:10); “For you will bless the ______. Yahweh, you will surround him with favor as with a shield.” (5:12, WEB) Hard
Answer: transgressions; righteous. The first passage reads “… by their own counsels; Thrust them out in the multitude of their transgressions, For they have rebelled against you.”; the second reads “For you will bless the righteous. Yahweh, you will surround him with favor as with a shield.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. True or false: The psalmist prayed to the LORD in the morning. (5:3) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 5:3 records: “Yahweh, in the morning you shall hear my voice. In the morning I will lay my requests before you, and will watch expectantly.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “You will destroy those who speak lies. Yahweh abhors the ______-thirsty and deceitful man.”? (5:6, WEB) Medium
A) tongue B) face C) arm D) blood
Answer: D. blood. The passage specifies blood: “You will destroy those who speak lies. Yahweh abhors the blood-thirsty and deceitful man.”
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… I will come into your house: I will bow toward your holy altar in reverence of you.” (5:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… I will come into your house: I will bow toward your holy temple in reverence of you.” It says temple, not altar.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For there is no ______ in their mouth. Their heart is destruction. Their throat is an open tomb. They flatter with their tongue.” (5:9); “… always shout for joy, because you defend them. Let them also who ______ your name be joyful in you.” (5:11, WEB) Hard
A) faithfulness; peace B) love; faithfulness C) anger; love D) faithfulness; love
Answer: D. faithfulness; love. Verse 9 reads “For there is no faithfulness in their mouth. Their heart is destruction. Their throat is an open tomb. They flatter with their tongue.”; verse 11 reads “… always shout for joy, because you defend them. Let them also who love your name be joyful in you.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For you are not a God who has ______ in wickedness. Evil ______ live with you.” (5:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: pleasure; can't. The wording is “For you are not a God who has pleasure in wickedness. Evil can't live with you.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… arrogant shall not stand in your sight. You hate all workers of ______.”? (5:5, WEB) Easy
A) wickedness B) deceit C) iniquity D) sin
Answer: C. iniquity. Psalms 5:5 uses iniquity: “… arrogant shall not stand in your sight. You hate all workers of iniquity.”
Psalms 6
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For in ______ there is no memory of you. In ______, who shall give you thanks?” (6:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: death; Sheol. The wording is “For in death there is no memory of you. In Sheol, who shall give you thanks?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “My eye wastes away because of ______; It grows old because of all my adversaries.”? (6:7, WEB) Medium
A) hope B) fear C) grief D) joy
Answer: C. grief. The passage specifies grief: “My eye wastes away because of grief; It grows old because of all my adversaries.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “May all my enemies be ashamed and dismayed. They shall ______ back, they shall be disgraced suddenly.” (6:10); “Yahweh has heard my ______. Yahweh accepts my prayer.” (6:9, WEB) Hard
A) disgraced; supplication B) supplication; turn C) turn; iniquity D) turn; supplication
Answer: D. turn; supplication. Verse 10 reads “May all my enemies be ashamed and dismayed. They shall turn back, they shall be disgraced suddenly.”; verse 9 reads “Yahweh has heard my supplication. Yahweh accepts my prayer.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Have ______ on me, Yahweh, for I am faint. Yahweh, heal me, for my bones are troubled.” (6:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: mercy. The cited words are “Have mercy on me, Yahweh, for I am faint. Yahweh, heal me, for my bones are troubled.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the psalmist said he was weary with his ______. (6:6) Easy
Answer: groaning. Psalms 6:6 records: “I am weary with my groaning; Every night I flood my bed; I drench my couch with my tears.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Return, Yahweh. ______ my soul, And save me for your loving kindness' sake.”? (6:4, WEB) Easy
A) save B) discipline C) weeping D) Deliver
Answer: D. Deliver. Psalms 6:4 uses Deliver: “Return, Yahweh. Deliver my soul, And save me for your loving kindness' sake.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “My soul is also in great ______. But you, Yahweh--how long?” (6:3); “Yahweh, don't rebuke me in your anger, Neither ______ me in your wrath.” (6:1, WEB) Hard
Answer: anguish; discipline. The first passage reads “My soul is also in great anguish. But you, Yahweh--how long?”; the second reads “Yahweh, don't rebuke me in your anger, Neither discipline me in your wrath.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Depart from me, all you workers of idolatry, For Yahweh has heard the voice of my weeping.” (6:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Depart from me, all you workers of iniquity, For Yahweh has heard the voice of my weeping.” It says iniquity, not idolatry.

Answer: deliver. The cited words are “… like a lion, Ripping it in pieces, while there is none to deliver.”
Q2. His violence shall come down on the crown of his own head.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Answer: rage; judgment. The wording is “… your anger. Lift up yourself against the rage of my adversaries. Awake for me. You have commanded judgment.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. True or false: David took refuge in the LORD and asked Him to judge justly. (7:1) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 7:1 records: “Yahweh, my God, I take refuge in you. Save me from all those who pursue me, and deliver me,”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Yahweh administers judgment to the peoples. Judge me, Yahweh, according to my ______, And to my integrity that is in me.”? (7:8, WEB) Medium

Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “God is a righteous ______, Yes, a God who has indignation every day.” (7:11); “If a man doesn't relent, he will sharpen his sword; He has ______ and strung his bow.” (7:12, WEB) Hard
A) judge; tread B) judge; bent C) bent; judge D) kings; bent
Answer: B. judge; bent. Verse 11 reads “God is a righteous judge, Yes, a God who has indignation every day.”; verse 12 reads “If a man doesn't relent, he will sharpen his sword; He has bent and strung his bow.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… But establish the righteous; Their minds and hearts are searched by the righteous God.” (7:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… But establish the righteous; Their minds and hearts are searched by the righteous God.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… enemy pursue my soul, and overtake it; Yes, let him tread my ______ down to the earth, And lay my glory in the dust. Selah.”? (7:5, WEB) Easy

Psalms 8
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Yahweh, our Lord, how ______ is your name in all the earth, Who has set your ______ above the heavens!” (8:1, WEB) Medium
Answer: majestic; glory. The wording is “Yahweh, our Lord, how majestic is your name in all the earth, Who has set your glory above the heavens!” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The birds of the sky, the fish of the sea, And stars passes through the paths of the seas.” (8:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The birds of the sky, the fish of the sea, And whatever passes through the paths of the seas.” It says whatever, not stars.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Yahweh, our Lord, How majestic is your ______ in all the earth!” (8:9); “… the sky, the fish of the sea, And whatever passes through the ______ of the seas.” (8:8, WEB) Hard
Answer: name; paths. The first passage reads “Yahweh, our Lord, How majestic is your name in all the earth!”; the second reads “… the sky, the fish of the sea, And whatever passes through the paths of the seas.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Answer: mindful. Psalms 8:4 records: “What is man, that you think of him? The son of man, that you care for him?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “From the lips of babes and ______ you have established strength, Because of your adversaries, that you might silence the enemy and the avenger.” (8:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: infants. The cited words are “From the lips of babes and infants you have established strength, Because of your adversaries, that you might silence the enemy and the avenger.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “All ______ and oxen, Yes, and the animals of the field,” (8:7); “… the works of your hands. You have put all things under his ______:” (8:6, WEB) Hard

Answer: C. sheep; feet. Verse 7 reads “All sheep and oxen, Yes, and the animals of the field,”; verse 6 reads “… the works of your hands. You have put all things under his feet:”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “When I consider your heavens, the work of your ______, The moon and the stars, which you have ordained;”? (8:3, WEB) Easy

Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… him a little lower than God, And crowned him with glory and ______.”? (8:5, WEB) Medium
A) kindness B) grief C) honor D) wrath
Answer: C. honor. The passage specifies honor: “… him a little lower than God, And crowned him with glory and honor.”

Answer: God. Psalms 14:1 records: “The fool has said in his heart, "There is no God." They are corrupt. They have done abominable works. There is none who does good.”

Psalms 22
Q1. Multiple choice: How should the psalm's suffering and its later use in the Gospels be held together? (22:1, 22-31; Matthew 27:46) Hard
A) The Gospels never draw on its language. B) First read the lament and movement toward praise, then consider its use in depicting Jesus' suffering. C) The original lament has no meaning at all. D) The psalm contains only despair and no praise.
Answer: B. First read the lament and movement toward praise, then consider its use in depicting Jesus' suffering.. Jesus takes the opening lament onto His lips. Christian interpretation need not erase the psalmist's own prayer or the psalm's ending in worship.
Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, psalm 22 begins, My God, my God, why have You ______ me? (22:1) Easy
Answer: forsaken. Psalms 22:1 records: “My God, my God, why have you forsaken me? Why are you so far from helping me, and from the words of my groaning?”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “"He ______ in Yahweh; Let him ______ him; Let him rescue him, since he delights in him."” (22:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: trusts; deliver. The wording is “"He trusts in Yahweh; Let him deliver him; Let him rescue him, since he delights in him."” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “They divide my garments among them. They cast lots for my ______.” (22:18); “______ me from the lion's mouth; Yes, from the horns of the wild oxen you have answered me.” (22:21, WEB) Hard
A) roaring; Save B) clothing; fear C) clothing; Save D) Save; clothing
Answer: C. clothing; Save. Verse 18 reads “They divide my garments among them. They cast lots for my clothing.”; verse 21 reads “Save me from the lion's mouth; Yes, from the horns of the wild oxen you have answered me.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… roof of my mouth. You have brought me into the dust of deliverance.” (22:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… roof of my mouth. You have brought me into the dust of death.” It says death, not deliverance.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “My God, I cry in the ______, but you don't answer; In the night season, and am not silent.” (22:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: daytime. The cited words are “My God, I cry in the daytime, but you don't answer; In the night season, and am not silent.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Many ______ have surrounded me. Strong bulls of Bashan have encircled me.”? (22:12, WEB) Medium
A) dove B) ox C) bulls D) doves
Answer: C. bulls. The passage specifies bulls: “Many bulls have surrounded me. Strong bulls of Bashan have encircled me.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… I am a worm, and no man; A reproach of men, and ______ by the people.”? (22:6, WEB) Easy

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q5.
