Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “"An evil disease," they say, "has afflicted him. Now that he ______ he shall rise up no more."” (41:8); “But you, Yahweh, have ______ on me, and raise me up, That I may repay them.” (41:10, WEB) Hard
A) goes; mercy B) lies; mercy C) mercy; lies D) lies; peace
Answer: B. lies; mercy. Verse 8 reads “"An evil disease," they say, "has afflicted him. Now that he lies he shall rise up no more."”; verse 10 reads “But you, Yahweh, have mercy on me, and raise me up, That I may repay them.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. True or false: The psalm says, "Blessed is he who considers the poor." (41:1) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 41:1 records: “Blessed is he who considers the poor: Yahweh will deliver him in the day of evil.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… enemies speak evil against me: "When will he die, and his name ______?"”? (41:5, WEB) Medium
A) perish B) lifted C) sickbed D) mercy
Answer: A. perish. The passage specifies perish: “… enemies speak evil against me: "When will he die, and his name perish?"”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “If he comes to see me, he speaks falsehood. His ______ gathers ______ to itself. When he goes abroad, he tells it.” (41:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: heart; iniquity. The wording is “If he comes to see me, he speaks falsehood. His heart gathers iniquity to itself. When he goes abroad, he tells it.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Yahweh will ______ him on his sickbed, And restore him from his bed of illness.”? (41:3, WEB) Easy
A) trusted B) sickbed C) Israel D) sustain
Answer: D. sustain. Psalms 41:3 uses sustain: “Yahweh will sustain him on his sickbed, And restore him from his bed of illness.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “By this I know that you ______ in me, Because my enemy doesn't triumph over me.” (41:11); “Blessed be Yahweh, the God of ______, From everlasting and to everlasting! Amen and amen.” (41:13, WEB) Hard
Answer: delight; Israel. The first passage reads “By this I know that you delight in me, Because my enemy doesn't triumph over me.”; the second reads “Blessed be Yahweh, the God of Israel, From everlasting and to everlasting! Amen and amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Yes, my own familiar friend, in whom I trusted, Who ate honey with me, Has lifted up his heel against me.” (41:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Yes, my own familiar friend, in whom I trusted, Who ate bread with me, Has lifted up his heel against me.” It says bread, not honey.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Yahweh will ______ him, and keep him alive, He shall be blessed on the earth, And he will not surrender him to the will of his …” (41:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: preserve. The cited words are “Yahweh will preserve him, and keep him alive, He shall be blessed on the earth, And he will not surrender him to the will of his …”
Psalms 42
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… song shall be with me: A prayer to the God of my ______.” (42:8); “I will ask God, my ______, "Why have you forgotten me? Why do I go mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?"” (42:9, WEB) Hard
A) life; rock B) deliverance; rock C) rock; life D) life; oppression
Answer: A. life; rock. Verse 8 reads “… song shall be with me: A prayer to the God of my life.”; verse 9 reads “I will ask God, my rock, "Why have you forgotten me? Why do I go mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?"”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… them to the house of God, With the voice of joy and ______, a multitude keeping a holy day.”? (42:4, WEB) Easy
A) praise B) rest C) prayer D) power
Answer: A. praise. Psalms 42:4 uses praise: “… them to the house of God, With the voice of joy and praise, a multitude keeping a holy day.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “My tears have been my food day and ______, While they continually ask me, "Where is your God?"” (42:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: night. The cited words are “My tears have been my food day and night, While they continually ask me, "Where is your God?"”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… deep at the noise of your waterfalls. All your waves and your thirsts have swept over me.” (42:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… deep at the noise of your waterfalls. All your waves and your billows have swept over me.” It says billows, not thirsts.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the psalmist said, As a deer pants for water, so my soul pants for ______. (42:1) Easy
Answer: God. Psalms 42:1 records: “As the deer pants for the water brooks, So my soul pants after you, God.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… are you disturbed within me? ______ in God! For I shall still praise him for the saving help of his ______.” (42:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: Hope; presence. The wording is “… are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him for the saving help of his presence.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… a sword in my bones, my adversaries reproach me, While they continually ______ me, "Where is your God?"” (42:10); “Why are you in despair, my soul? Why are you ______ within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him, The saving help of …” (42:11, WEB) Hard
Answer: ask; disturbed. The first passage reads “… a sword in my bones, my adversaries reproach me, While they continually ask me, "Where is your God?"”; the second reads “Why are you in despair, my soul? Why are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him, The saving help of …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… despair within me. Therefore I remember you from the land of the ______, The heights of Hermon, from the hill Mizar.”? (42:6, WEB) Medium
A) Edom B) Jordan C) Samaria D) Moab
Answer: B. Jordan. The passage specifies Jordan: “… despair within me. Therefore I remember you from the land of the Jordan, The heights of Hermon, from the hill Mizar.”
Psalms 43
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still ______ him: My Savior, my helper, and my God.”? (43:5, WEB) Medium
A) curse B) power C) death D) praise
Answer: D. praise. The passage specifies praise: “… are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him: My Savior, my helper, and my God.”
Q2. True or false: The psalmist asked God to send out His light and truth. (43:3) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 43:3 records: “Oh, send out your light and your truth. Let them lead me. Let them bring me to your holy hill, To your tents.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “For you are the God of my strength. Why have you ______ me? Why do I go mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?”? (43:2, WEB) Easy
A) rejected B) fatherless C) light D) strangers
Answer: A. rejected. Psalms 43:2 uses rejected: “For you are the God of my strength. Why have you rejected me? Why do I go mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Oh, send out your ungodly and your truth. Let them lead me. Let them bring me to your holy hill, To your tents.” (43:3, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Oh, send out your light and your truth. Let them lead me. Let them bring me to your holy hill, To your tents.” It says light, not ungodly.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Vindicate me, God, and plead my cause against an ungodly nation. Oh, ______ me from deceitful and wicked men.” (43:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: deliver. The cited words are “Vindicate me, God, and plead my cause against an ungodly nation. Oh, deliver me from deceitful and wicked men.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Then I will go to the ______ of God, To God, my exceeding ______. I will praise you on the harp, God, my God.” (43:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: altar; joy. The wording is “Then I will go to the altar of God, To God, my exceeding joy. I will praise you on the harp, God, my God.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Why are you in despair, my soul? Why are you ______ within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him: My Savior, my helper, …” (43:5); “Vindicate me, God, and plead my cause against an ungodly ______. Oh, deliver me from deceitful and wicked men.” (43:1, WEB) Hard
Answer: disturbed; nation. The first passage reads “Why are you in despair, my soul? Why are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him: My Savior, my helper, …”; the second reads “Vindicate me, God, and plead my cause against an ungodly nation. Oh, deliver me from deceitful and wicked men.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Oh, send out your light and your ______. Let them lead me. Let them bring me to your holy hill, To your tents.” (43:3); “… are you in despair, my soul? Why are you disturbed within me? ______ in God! For I shall still praise him: My Savior, my helper, and …” (43:5, WEB) Hard
A) Hope; truth B) truth; shame C) truth; Hope D) love; Hope
Answer: C. truth; Hope. Verse 3 reads “Oh, send out your light and your truth. Let them lead me. Let them bring me to your holy hill, To your tents.”; verse 5 reads “… are you in despair, my soul? Why are you disturbed within me? Hope in God! For I shall still praise him: My Savior, my helper, and …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Psalms 44
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… down our adversaries. Through your name, will we tread them under who ______ up against us.”? (44:5, WEB) Easy
A) rise B) push C) light D) soul
Answer: A. rise. Psalms 44:5 uses rise: “… down our adversaries. Through your name, will we tread them under who rise up against us.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Won't God search this out? For he knows the ______ of the heart.” (44:21); “Wake up! Why do you sleep, Lord? Arise! Don't reject us ______.” (44:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: secrets; forever. The first passage reads “Won't God search this out? For he knows the secrets of the heart.”; the second reads “Wake up! Why do you sleep, Lord? Arise! Don't reject us forever.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “In God we have made our ______ all day long, We will give ______ to your name forever. Selah.” (44:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: boast; thanks. The wording is “In God we have made our boast all day long, We will give thanks to your name forever. Selah.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “At the taunt of one who reproaches and ______ abuses, Because of the enemy and the avenger.” (44:16); “Our ______ has not turned back, Neither have our steps strayed from your path,” (44:18, WEB) Hard
A) verbally; heart B) death; heart C) heart; verbally D) verbally; tongue
Answer: A. verbally; heart. Verse 16 reads “At the taunt of one who reproaches and verbally abuses, Because of the enemy and the avenger.”; verse 18 reads “Our heart has not turned back, Neither have our steps strayed from your path,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You drove out the ______ with your hand, But you planted them. You afflicted the peoples, But you spread them abroad.” (44:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: nations. The cited words are “You drove out the nations with your hand, But you planted them. You afflicted the peoples, But you spread them abroad.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “You make us a reproach to our neighbors, A scoffing and a derision to those who are around us.” (44:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “You make us a reproach to our neighbors, A scoffing and a derision to those who are around us.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the psalmist said that God's people were given over like sheep for the ______. (44:22) Easy
Answer: slaughter. Psalms 44:22 records: “Yes, for your sake we are killed all day long. We are regarded as sheep for the slaughter.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… make us turn back from the adversary. Those who hate us take ______ for themselves.”? (44:10, WEB) Medium
A) body B) spoil C) adversaries D) killed
Answer: B. spoil. The passage specifies spoil: “… make us turn back from the adversary. Those who hate us take spoil for themselves.”
Psalms 45
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “In your majesty ride on victoriously on behalf of truth, humility, and ______. Let your right hand display awesome deeds.”? (45:4, WEB) Easy
A) shame B) anger C) righteousness D) kindness
Answer: C. righteousness. Psalms 45:4 uses righteousness: “In your majesty ride on victoriously on behalf of truth, humility, and righteousness. Let your right hand display awesome deeds.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You are the most excellent of the sons of men. ______ has anointed your lips, Therefore God has blessed you forever.” (45:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Grace. The cited words are “You are the most excellent of the sons of men. Grace has anointed your lips, Therefore God has blessed you forever.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and hated wickedness. Therefore God, your God, has anointed you with the ______ of gladness above your fellows.”? (45:7, WEB) Medium
A) water B) oil C) pomegranates D) bread
Answer: B. oil. The passage specifies oil: “… and hated wickedness. Therefore God, your God, has anointed you with the oil of gladness above your fellows.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Listen, daughter, consider, and ______ your ear. Forget your own people, and also your father's house.” (45:10); “The ______ of Tyre comes with a gift. The rich among the people entreat your favor.” (45:12, WEB) Hard
A) turn; brothers B) daughter; turn C) Forget; daughter D) turn; daughter
Answer: D. turn; daughter. Verse 10 reads “Listen, daughter, consider, and turn your ear. Forget your own people, and also your father's house.”; verse 12 reads “The daughter of Tyre comes with a gift. The rich among the people entreat your favor.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. True or false: Psalm 45 is a song about a royal wedding. (45:1) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 45:1 records: “My heart overflows with a noble theme. I recite my verses for the king. My tongue is like the pen of a skillful writer.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… shall be led to the king in embroidered work. The virgins, her ______ who follow her, shall be brought to you.” (45:14); “With gladness and rejoicing they shall be ______. They shall enter into the king's palace.” (45:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: companions; led. The first passage reads “… shall be led to the king in embroidered work. The virgins, her companions who follow her, shall be brought to you.”; the second reads “With gladness and rejoicing they shall be led. They shall enter into the king's palace.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Your arrows are sharp. The ______ fall under you, with arrows in the ______ of the king's enemies.” (45:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: nations; heart. The wording is “Your arrows are sharp. The nations fall under you, with arrows in the heart of the king's enemies.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “prophets' daughters are among your honorable women. At your right hand the queen stands in gold of Ophir.” (45:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Kings' daughters are among your honorable women. At your right hand the queen stands in gold of Ophir.” It says Kings, not prophets.
Psalms 46
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “There is a ______, the streams of which make the city of God glad, The ______ place of the tents of the Most High.” (46:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: river; holy. The wording is “There is a river, the streams of which make the city of God glad, The holy place of the tents of the Most High.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, psalm 46 says, God is our refuge and ______. (46:1) Easy
Answer: strength. Psalms 46:1 records: “God is our refuge and strength, A very present help in trouble.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Yahweh of Hosts is with us. The God of Jacob is our offerings. Selah.” (46:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Yahweh of Hosts is with us. The God of Jacob is our refuge. Selah.” It says refuge, not offerings.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… afraid, though the earth changes, Though the mountains are shaken into the ______ of the seas;” (46:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: heart. The cited words are “… afraid, though the earth changes, Though the mountains are shaken into the heart of the seas;”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The ______ raged. The kingdoms were moved. He lifted his voice, and the earth melted.”? (46:6, WEB) Medium
A) streams B) makes C) nations D) works
Answer: C. nations. The passage specifies nations: “The nations raged. The kingdoms were moved. He lifted his voice, and the earth melted.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… it roar and are troubled, Though the mountains tremble with their swelling. ______.”? (46:3, WEB) Easy
A) dawn B) know C) Selah D) afraid
Answer: C. Selah. Psalms 46:3 uses Selah: “… it roar and are troubled, Though the mountains tremble with their swelling. Selah.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… am God. I will be exalted among the nations. I will be ______ in the earth."” (46:10); “Yahweh of Hosts is with us. The God of ______ is our refuge. Selah.” (46:11, WEB) Hard
Answer: exalted; Jacob. The first passage reads “… am God. I will be exalted among the nations. I will be exalted in the earth."”; the second reads “Yahweh of Hosts is with us. The God of Jacob is our refuge. Selah.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the bow, and shatters the spear. He burns the chariots in the ______.” (46:9); “Come, see Yahweh's ______, What desolations he has made in the earth.” (46:8, WEB) Hard
A) waters; works B) fire; mountains C) fire; works D) works; fire
Answer: C. fire; works. Verse 9 reads “… the bow, and shatters the spear. He burns the chariots in the fire.”; verse 8 reads “Come, see Yahweh's works, What desolations he has made in the earth.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For God is the King of all the earth. Sing praises with understanding.” (47:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “For God is the King of all the earth. Sing praises with understanding.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He chooses our inheritance for us, The glory of ______ whom he loved. Selah.”? (47:4, WEB) Easy
A) Cain B) Jacob C) Saul D) Judah
Answer: B. Jacob. Psalms 47:4 uses Jacob: “He chooses our inheritance for us, The glory of Jacob whom he loved. Selah.”
Q4. True or false: God is the great King over all the earth. (47:2) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 47:2 records: “For Yahweh Most High is awesome. He is a great King over all the earth.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He subdues nations under us, And ______ under our feet.” (47:3); “… are gathered together, The people of the God of Abraham. For the ______ of the earth belong to God. He is greatly exalted!” (47:9, WEB) Hard
Answer: peoples; shields. The first passage reads “He subdues nations under us, And peoples under our feet.”; the second reads “… are gathered together, The people of the God of Abraham. For the shields of the earth belong to God. He is greatly exalted!”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… of the peoples are gathered together, The people of the God of ______. For the shields of the earth belong to God. He is greatly exalted!” (47:9); “God reigns over the nations. God sits on his ______ throne.” (47:8, WEB) Hard

He is greatly exalted!”; verse 8 reads “God reigns over the nations. God sits on his holy throne.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Answer: praise; King. The wording is “Sing praise to God, sing praises. Sing praises to our King, sing praises.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “God has gone up with a shout, Yahweh with the ______ of a trumpet.”? (47:5, WEB) Medium
A) voice B) sound C) sits D) King
Answer: B. sound. The passage specifies sound: “God has gone up with a shout, Yahweh with the sound of a trumpet.”

Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Don't be afraid when a man is made rich, When the ______ of his house is increased.” (49:16); “Though while he lived he blessed his soul-- And men ______ you when you do well for yourself--” (49:18, WEB) Hard

Psalms 50
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Offer to God the ______ of thanksgiving. Pay your vows to the Most High.” (50:14); “… do you have to declare my statutes, That you have taken my ______ on your lips,” (50:16, WEB) Hard
A) curse; covenant B) sacrifice; covenant C) sacrifice; commandments D) covenant; sacrifice
Answer: B. sacrifice; covenant. Verse 14 reads “Offer to God the sacrifice of thanksgiving. Pay your vows to the Most High.”; verse 16 reads “… do you have to declare my statutes, That you have taken my covenant on your lips,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “I have no need for a bull from your stall, Nor ______ goats from your pens.”? (50:9, WEB) Medium
A) calls B) thousand C) beauty D) male
Answer: D. male. The passage specifies male: “I have no need for a bull from your stall, Nor male goats from your pens.”

I will rebuke you, and accuse you in front of your eyes.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Psalms 51
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Wash me thoroughly from my iniquity. Cleanse me from my ______.” (51:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: sin. The cited words are “Wash me thoroughly from my iniquity. Cleanse me from my sin.”
Q2. Multiple choice: What does the contrast between sacrifice and a broken spirit emphasize? (51:16-19) Hard
A) The psalm praises pride. B) The prayer says wrongdoing has no effect on others. C) Ritual cannot substitute for genuine repentance. D) Repentance is unnecessary if an offering is expensive.
Answer: C. Ritual cannot substitute for genuine repentance.. The prayer values a contrite heart and then speaks of right sacrifices. It criticizes empty ritual rather than claiming that the whole sacrificial system was meaningless.
Q3. True or false: David asked God to create in him a clean heart. (51:10) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 51:10 records: “Create in me a clean heart, O God. Renew a right spirit within me.”

Answer: D. justified. Psalms 51:4 uses justified: “… your sight; That you may be proved right when you speak, And justified when you judge.”

Answer: B. salvation; righteousness. Verse 12 reads “Restore to me the joy of your salvation. Uphold me with a willing spirit.”; verse 14 reads “… the God of my salvation. My tongue shall sing aloud of your righteousness.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He also drove out the nations before them, Allotted them for an ______ by line, And made the tribes of Israel to dwell in their tents.” (78:55); “______ devoured their young men; Their virgins had no wedding song.” (78:63, WEB) Hard
Answer: inheritance; Fire. The first passage reads “He also drove out the nations before them, Allotted them for an inheritance by line, And made the tribes of Israel to dwell in their tents.”; the second reads “Fire devoured their young men; Their virgins had no wedding song.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “When he killed them, then they inquired after him. They returned and afraid God earnestly.” (78:34, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “When he killed them, then they inquired after him. They returned and sought God earnestly.” It says sought, not afraid.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For he established a testimony in ______, And appointed a law in Israel, Which he commanded our fathers, That they should make them known to their …” (78:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jacob. The cited words are “For he established a testimony in Jacob, And appointed a law in Israel, Which he commanded our fathers, That they should make them known to their …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, psalm 78 tells the story of Israel's history in order to teach the ______ generation. (78:6) Easy
Answer: next. Psalms 78:6 records: “That the generation to come might know, even the children who should be born; Who should arise and tell their children,”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “He caused the east wind to ______ in the sky. By his power he guided the south wind.”? (78:26, WEB) Medium
A) blow B) full C) power D) skies
Answer: A. blow. The passage specifies blow: “He caused the east wind to blow in the sky. By his power he guided the south wind.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… marvelous things in the sight of their fathers, In the land of ______, in the field of Zoan.”? (78:12, WEB) Easy
A) Zion B) Jericho C) Egypt D) Nineveh
Answer: C. Egypt. Psalms 78:12 uses Egypt: “… marvelous things in the sight of their fathers, In the land of Egypt, in the field of Zoan.”

Answer: bread; provide. The wording is “… so that waters gushed out, Streams overflowed. Can he give bread also? Will he provide flesh for his people?"” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: C. sing; born. Verse 7 reads “Those who sing as well as those who dance say, "All my springs are in you."”; verse 6 reads “Yahweh will count, when he writes up the peoples, "This one was born there." Selah.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Answer: True. The actual wording is “Yahweh will count, when he writes up the peoples, "This one was born there." Selah.”

Psalms 106
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “They soon forgot his works. They didn't ______ for his ______,” (106:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: wait; counsel. The wording is “They soon forgot his works. They didn't wait for his counsel,” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Blessed are those who keep ______. Blessed is one who does what is right at all times.” (106:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: justice. The cited words are “Blessed are those who keep justice. Blessed is one who does what is right at all times.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “A ______ was kindled in their company. The flame burned up the wicked.”? (106:18, WEB) Medium
A) wickedly B) Savior C) plague D) fire
Answer: D. fire. The passage specifies fire: “A fire was kindled in their company. The flame burned up the wicked.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Our fathers didn't understand your wonders in ______. They didn't remember the multitude of your loving kindnesses, But were rebellious at the sea, even at the …”? (106:7, WEB) Easy
A) Babylon B) Egypt C) Jordan D) Gilead
Answer: B. Egypt. Psalms 106:7 uses Egypt: “Our fathers didn't understand your wonders in Egypt. They didn't remember the multitude of your loving kindnesses, But were rebellious at the sea, even at the …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “They shed innocent blood, Even the ______ of their sons and of their daughters, Whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan. The land was polluted …” (106:38); “Their enemies also ______ them. They were brought into subjection under their hand.” (106:42, WEB) Hard
Answer: blood; oppressed. The first passage reads “They shed innocent blood, Even the blood of their sons and of their daughters, Whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan. The land was polluted …”; the second reads “Their enemies also oppressed them. They were brought into subjection under their hand.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “That he would overthrow their seed among the ______, And scatter them in the lands.” (106:27); “They angered him also at the ______ of Meribah, So that Moses was troubled for their sakes;” (106:32, WEB) Hard
A) lands; waters B) waters; nations C) nations; waters D) nations; deeds
Answer: C. nations; waters. Verse 27 reads “That he would overthrow their seed among the nations, And scatter them in the lands.”; verse 32 reads “They angered him also at the waters of Meribah, So that Moses was troubled for their sakes;”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, oh give thanks to the LORD, for He is ______; His steadfast love endures forever. (106:1) Easy
Answer: good. Psalms 106:1 records: “Praise Yahweh! Give thanks to Yahweh, for he is good, For his loving kindness endures forever.”
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Therefore he said that he would destroy them, Had Moses, his chosen, not stood before him in the breach, To turn away his wrath, so that …” (106:23, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Therefore he said that he would destroy them, Had Moses, his chosen, not stood before him in the breach, To turn away his wrath, so that …”
Psalms 107
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Let them praise Yahweh for his loving kindness, For his wonderful works to the children of men!” (107:21, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Let them praise Yahweh for his loving kindness, For his wonderful works to the children of men!”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Therefore he brought down their ______ with ______. They fell down, and there was none to help.” (107:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: heart; labor. The wording is “Therefore he brought down their heart with labor. They fell down, and there was none to help.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Let them ______ Yahweh for his loving kindness, For his wonderful works to the children of men!”? (107:8, WEB) Easy
A) life B) redemption C) praise D) refuge
Answer: C. praise. Psalms 107:8 uses praise: “Let them praise Yahweh for his loving kindness, For his wonderful works to the children of men!”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He turns rivers into a desert, ______ springs into a thirsty ground,” (107:33); “______ fields, plant vineyards, And reap the fruits of increase.” (107:37, WEB) Hard
Answer: Water; Sow. The first passage reads “He turns rivers into a desert, Water springs into a thirsty ground,”; the second reads “Sow fields, plant vineyards, And reap the fruits of increase.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For he commands, and ______ the stormy wind, Which lifts up its waves.” (107:25); “He makes the storm a ______, So that its waves are still.” (107:29, WEB) Hard
A) raises; calm B) calm; raises C) sends; calm D) raises; bound
Answer: A. raises; calm. Verse 25 reads “For he commands, and raises the stormy wind, Which lifts up its waves.”; verse 29 reads “He makes the storm a calm, So that its waves are still.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “For he has broken the gates of ______, And cut through bars of iron.”? (107:16, WEB) Medium
A) brass B) stones C) wood D) clay
Answer: A. brass. The passage specifies brass: “For he has broken the gates of brass, And cut through bars of iron.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “And gathered out of the lands, From the east and from the ______, From the north and from the south.” (107:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: west. The cited words are “And gathered out of the lands, From the east and from the west, From the north and from the south.”
Q8. True or false: The psalm says, "Let the redeemed of the LORD say so." (107:2) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 107:2 records: “Let the redeemed by Yahweh say so, Whom he has redeemed from the hand of the adversary,”
Psalms 108
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… "In triumph, I will divide Shechem, and measure out the valley of ______.”? (108:7, WEB) Medium
A) fortified B) Succoth C) sanctuary D) Ephraim
Answer: B. Succoth. The passage specifies Succoth: “… "In triumph, I will divide Shechem, and measure out the valley of Succoth.”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Shiloh is mine. Manasseh is mine. Ephraim also is my helmet. Judah is my scepter.” (108:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Gilead is mine. Manasseh is mine. Ephraim also is my helmet. Judah is my scepter.” It says Gilead, not Shiloh.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Be ______, God, above the heavens, Let your ______ be over all the earth.” (108:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: exalted; glory. The wording is “Be exalted, God, above the heavens, Let your glory be over all the earth.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Wake up, harp and lyre! I will ______ up the dawn.” (108:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: wake. The cited words are “Wake up, harp and lyre! I will wake up the dawn.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the psalmist said his heart is steadfast, and he will sing and make ______. (108:1) Easy
Answer: music. Psalms 108:1 records: “My heart is steadfast, God. I will sing and I will make music with my soul.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Moab is my wash pot. I will toss my sandal on ______. I will shout over Philistia."” (108:9); “Who will bring me into the ______ city? Who has led me to Edom?” (108:10, WEB) Hard
A) Edom; fortified B) fortified; Edom C) Egypt; fortified D) Edom; delivered
Answer: A. Edom; fortified. Verse 9 reads “Moab is my wash pot. I will toss my sandal on Edom. I will shout over Philistia."”; verse 10 reads “Who will bring me into the fortified city? Who has led me to Edom?”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “For your loving kindness is great above the heavens. Your ______ reaches to the skies.”? (108:4, WEB) Easy
A) justice B) joy C) anger D) faithfulness
Answer: D. faithfulness. Psalms 108:4 uses faithfulness: “For your loving kindness is great above the heavens. Your faithfulness reaches to the skies.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Haven't you rejected us, God? You don't go forth, God, with our ______.” (108:11); “Give us help against the ______, For the help of man is vain.” (108:12, WEB) Hard
Answer: armies; enemy. The first passage reads “Haven't you rejected us, God? You don't go forth, God, with our armies.”; the second reads “Give us help against the enemy, For the help of man is vain.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Psalms 109
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Because he didn't remember to show kindness, But persecuted the poor and needy man, The broken in heart, to kill them.” (109:16, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Because he didn't remember to show kindness, But persecuted the poor and needy man, The broken in heart, to kill them.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Set a ______ man over him. Let an adversary stand at his right hand.”? (109:6, WEB) Easy
A) obedient B) pure C) clean D) wicked
Answer: D. wicked. Psalms 109:6 uses wicked: “Set a wicked man over him. Let an adversary stand at his right hand.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… mouth of deceit against me. They have spoken to me with a ______ tongue.” (109:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: lying. The cited words are “… mouth of deceit against me. They have spoken to me with a lying tongue.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Let there be none to extend ______ to him, Neither let there be any to have pity on his fatherless children.”? (109:12, WEB) Medium
A) justice B) kindness C) joy D) righteousness
Answer: B. kindness. The passage specifies kindness: “Let there be none to extend kindness to him, Neither let there be any to have pity on his fatherless children.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… with his garment. It came into his inward parts like water, Like ______ into his bones.” (109:18); “I fade away like an evening ______. I am shaken off like a locust.” (109:23, WEB) Hard
A) shadow; oil B) oil; shadow C) salt; shadow D) oil; inward
Answer: B. oil; shadow. Verse 18 reads “… with his garment. It came into his inward parts like water, Like oil into his bones.”; verse 23 reads “I fade away like an evening shadow. I am shaken off like a locust.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Let his children be ______, And his wife a ______.” (109:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: fatherless; widow. The wording is “Let his children be fatherless, And his wife a widow.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “My ______ are weak through fasting. My body is thin and lacks fat.” (109:24); “… but you bless. When they arise, they will be shamed, But your ______ shall rejoice.” (109:28, WEB) Hard
Answer: knees; servant. The first passage reads “My knees are weak through fasting. My body is thin and lacks fat.”; the second reads “… but you bless. When they arise, they will be shamed, But your servant shall rejoice.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. True or false: The psalmist asked God to act for him for the sake of His name. (109:21) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 109:21 records: “But deal with me, Yahweh the Lord, for your name's sake, Because your loving kindness is good, deliver me;”
Psalms 110
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “He will drink of the brook in the way; Therefore he will ______ up his head.” (110:7); “He will judge among the nations. He will heap up dead ______. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.” (110:6, WEB) Hard
A) lift; nations B) lift; bodies C) whole; bodies D) bodies; lift
Answer: B. lift; bodies. Verse 7 reads “He will drink of the brook in the way; Therefore he will lift up his head.”; verse 6 reads “He will judge among the nations. He will heap up dead bodies. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “He will judge among the ______. He will heap up dead bodies. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.”? (110:6, WEB) Medium
A) enemies B) nations C) kings D) bodies
Answer: B. nations. The passage specifies nations: “He will judge among the nations. He will heap up dead bodies. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Your people offer themselves willingly in the day of your power, in ______ array. Out of the womb of the morning, you have the dew of …” (110:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: holy. The cited words are “Your people offer themselves willingly in the day of your power, in holy array. Out of the womb of the morning, you have the dew of …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He will ______ among the nations. He will heap up dead bodies. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.” (110:6); “He will ______ of the brook in the way; Therefore he will lift up his head.” (110:7, WEB) Hard
Answer: judge; drink. The first passage reads “He will judge among the nations. He will heap up dead bodies. He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.”; the second reads “He will drink of the brook in the way; Therefore he will lift up his head.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The Lord is at your right hand. He will crush fathers in the day of his wrath.” (110:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The Lord is at your right hand. He will crush kings in the day of his wrath.” It says kings, not fathers.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Yahweh will send forth the rod of your strength out of ______. Rule in the midst of your enemies.”? (110:2, WEB) Easy
A) Zion B) Egypt C) Canaan D) Shiloh
Answer: A. Zion. Psalms 110:2 uses Zion: “Yahweh will send forth the rod of your strength out of Zion. Rule in the midst of your enemies.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Yahweh has ______, and will not change his mind: "You are a ______ forever in the order of Melchizedek."” (110:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: sworn; priest. The wording is “Yahweh has sworn, and will not change his mind: "You are a priest forever in the order of Melchizedek."” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the LORD said to my Lord, Sit at my ______. (110:1) Easy
Answer: right hand. Psalms 110:1 records: “Yahweh says to my Lord, "Sit at my right hand, Until I make your enemies your footstool for your feet."”
Psalms 111
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ Yah! I will give thanks to Yahweh with my whole heart, In the council of the upright, and in the congregation.” (111:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Praise. The cited words are “Praise Yah! I will give thanks to Yahweh with my whole heart, In the council of the upright, and in the congregation.”
Q2. True or false: The psalm says the fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom. (111:10) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 111:10 records: “The fear of Yahweh is the beginning of wisdom. All those who do his work have a good understanding. His praise endures forever!”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… the power of his works, In giving them the heritage of the righteousness.” (111:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… the power of his works, In giving them the heritage of the nations.” It says nations, not righteousness.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… has given food to those who fear him. He always remembers his ______.”? (111:5, WEB) Medium
A) covenant B) promises C) word D) words
Answer: A. covenant. The passage specifies covenant: “… has given food to those who fear him. He always remembers his covenant.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “They are established forever and ever. They are done in ______ and uprightness.” (111:8); “Yahweh's works are great, ______ by all those who delight in them.” (111:2, WEB) Hard
Answer: truth; Pondered. The first passage reads “They are established forever and ever. They are done in truth and uprightness.”; the second reads “Yahweh's works are great, Pondered by all those who delight in them.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “His work is honor and majesty. His ______ endures forever.”? (111:3, WEB) Easy
A) faithfulness B) understanding C) righteousness D) peace
Answer: C. righteousness. Psalms 111:3 uses righteousness: “His work is honor and majesty. His righteousness endures forever.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “He has ______ his wonderful works to be remembered. Yahweh is gracious and ______.” (111:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: caused; merciful. The wording is “He has caused his wonderful works to be remembered. Yahweh is gracious and merciful.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The works of his hands are truth and ______. All his precepts are sure.” (111:7); “He has sent ______ to his people. He has ordained his covenant forever. His name is holy and awesome!” (111:9, WEB) Hard
A) faithfulness; redemption B) justice; rest C) justice; redemption D) redemption; justice
Answer: C. justice; redemption. Verse 7 reads “The works of his hands are truth and justice. All his precepts are sure.”; verse 9 reads “He has sent redemption to his people. He has ordained his covenant forever. His name is holy and awesome!”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Psalms 112
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “He will not be afraid of evil news. His ______ is steadfast, trusting in Yahweh.” (112:7); “He has dispersed, he has given to the ______. His righteousness endures forever. His horn will be exalted with honor.” (112:9, WEB) Hard
A) heart; faithful B) poor; heart C) mouth; poor D) heart; poor
Answer: D. heart; poor. Verse 7 reads “He will not be afraid of evil news. His heart is steadfast, trusting in Yahweh.”; verse 9 reads “He has dispersed, he has given to the poor. His righteousness endures forever. His horn will be exalted with honor.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “For he will never be shaken. The righteous will be remembered forever.” (112:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “For he will never be shaken. The righteous will be remembered forever.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Wealth and ______ are in his house. His ______ endures forever.” (112:3, WEB) Medium
Answer: riches; righteousness. The wording is “Wealth and riches are in his house. His righteousness endures forever.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “The wicked will see it, and be ______. He shall gnash with his teeth, and melt away. The desire of the wicked will perish.” (112:10); “… He will not be afraid in the end when he sees his ______.” (112:8, WEB) Hard
Answer: grieved; adversaries. The first passage reads “The wicked will see it, and be grieved. He shall gnash with his teeth, and melt away. The desire of the wicked will perish.”; the second reads “… He will not be afraid in the end when he sees his adversaries.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, blessed is the man who fears the LORD and greatly delights in His ______. (112:1) Easy
Answer: commandments. Psalms 112:1 records: “Praise Yah! Blessed is the man who fears Yahweh, Who delights greatly in his commandments.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “His seed will be mighty in the ______. The generation of the upright will be blessed.” (112:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: land. The cited words are “His seed will be mighty in the land. The generation of the upright will be blessed.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… man who deals graciously and lends. He will maintain his cause in ______.”? (112:5, WEB) Medium
A) judgment B) commandment C) testimonies D) testimony
Answer: A. judgment. The passage specifies judgment: “… man who deals graciously and lends. He will maintain his cause in judgment.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “______ dawns in the darkness for the upright, Gracious, merciful, and righteous.”? (112:4, WEB) Easy
A) land B) perish C) Light D) dispersed
Answer: C. Light. Psalms 112:4 uses Light: “Light dawns in the darkness for the upright, Gracious, merciful, and righteous.”
Psalms 113
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “That he may set him with princes, Even with the ______ of his people.” (113:8); “He settles the barren woman in her home, As a joyful ______ of children. Praise Yah!” (113:9, WEB) Hard
Answer: princes; mother. The first passage reads “That he may set him with princes, Even with the princes of his people.”; the second reads “He settles the barren woman in her home, As a joyful mother of children. Praise Yah!”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ Yah! Praise, you servants of Yahweh, Praise the name of Yahweh.” (113:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Praise. The cited words are “Praise Yah! Praise, you servants of Yahweh, Praise the name of Yahweh.”
Q3. True or false: The psalm says the LORD is high above all nations. (113:4) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 113:4 records: “Yahweh is high above all nations, His glory above the heavens.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “He raises up the poor out of the dust. Lifts up the ______ from the ash heap;”? (113:7, WEB) Medium
A) heaven B) needy C) rising D) children
Answer: B. needy. The passage specifies needy: “He raises up the poor out of the dust. Lifts up the needy from the ash heap;”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Who ______ down to see in heaven and in the earth?” (113:6); “He settles the barren woman in her ______, As a joyful mother of children. Praise Yah!” (113:9, WEB) Hard
A) Lifts; home B) stoops; above C) home; stoops D) stoops; home
Answer: D. stoops; home. Verse 6 reads “Who stoops down to see in heaven and in the earth?”; verse 9 reads “He settles the barren woman in her home, As a joyful mother of children. Praise Yah!”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “From the ______ of the sun to the going down of the same, Yahweh's name is to be praised.”? (113:3, WEB) Easy
A) rising B) statutes C) offerings D) heart
Answer: A. rising. Psalms 113:3 uses rising: “From the rising of the sun to the going down of the same, Yahweh's name is to be praised.”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Who is like Yahweh, our God, Who has his tent on high,” (113:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Who is like Yahweh, our God, Who has his seat on high,” It says seat, not tent.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “______ be the name of Yahweh, From this time forth and ______.” (113:2, WEB) Medium
Answer: Blessed; forevermore. The wording is “Blessed be the name of Yahweh, From this time forth and forevermore.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Psalms 114
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “What was it, you sea, that you fled? You Jordan, that you turned back?” (114:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “What was it, you sea, that you fled? You Jordan, that you turned back?”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Who turned the rock into a pool of ______, The flint into a spring of waters.” (114:8); “Tremble, you earth, at the presence of the Lord, At the ______ of the God of Jacob,” (114:7, WEB) Hard
A) wine; presence B) presence; water C) water; presence D) water; hills
Answer: C. water; presence. Verse 8 reads “Who turned the rock into a pool of water, The flint into a spring of waters.”; verse 7 reads “Tremble, you earth, at the presence of the Lord, At the presence of the God of Jacob,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “______, you earth, at the presence of the Lord, At the presence of the God of Jacob,” (114:7); “… turned the rock into a pool of water, The flint into a ______ of waters.” (114:8, WEB) Hard
Answer: Tremble; spring. The first passage reads “Tremble, you earth, at the presence of the Lord, At the presence of the God of Jacob,”; the second reads “… turned the rock into a pool of water, The flint into a spring of waters.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “You mountains, that you skipped like rams; You little ______, like lambs?”? (114:6, WEB) Medium
A) waters B) hills C) mountains D) rams
Answer: B. hills. The passage specifies hills: “You mountains, that you skipped like rams; You little hills, like lambs?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “When Israel went forth out of Egypt, The house of ______ from a people of foreign language;” (114:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jacob. The cited words are “When Israel went forth out of Egypt, The house of Jacob from a people of foreign language;”
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, psalm 114 says, Judah became His sanctuary, Israel His ______. (114:2) Easy
Answer: dominion. Psalms 114:2 records: “Judah became his sanctuary, Israel his dominion.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “The sea saw it, and fled. The ______ was driven back.”? (114:3, WEB) Easy
A) Beersheba B) Canaan C) Jordan D) Persia
Answer: C. Jordan. Psalms 114:3 uses Jordan: “The sea saw it, and fled. The Jordan was driven back.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The ______ ______ like rams, The little hills like lambs.” (114:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: mountains; skipped. The wording is “The mountains skipped like rams, The little hills like lambs.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Psalms 115
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Not to us, Yahweh, not to us, But to your name give ______, For your loving kindness, and for your truth's sake.” (115:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: glory. The cited words are “Not to us, Yahweh, not to us, But to your name give glory, For your loving kindness, and for your truth's sake.”
Q2. True or false: The psalm says idols have mouths but cannot speak. (115:5) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 115:5 records: “They have mouths, but they don't speak; They have eyes, but they don't see;”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “They have ______, but they don't hear; They have ______, but they don't smell;” (115:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: ears; noses. The wording is “They have ears, but they don't hear; They have noses, but they don't smell;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… will bless the house of Israel. He will bless the house of ______.” (115:12); “He will bless those who ______ Yahweh, Both small and great.” (115:13, WEB) Hard
A) fear; Aaron B) Aaron; fear C) Naomi; fear D) Aaron; righteousness
Answer: B. Aaron; fear. Verse 12 reads “… will bless the house of Israel. He will bless the house of Aaron.”; verse 13 reads “He will bless those who fear Yahweh, Both small and great.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… have feet, but they don't walk; Neither do they speak through their ______.”? (115:7, WEB) Medium
A) throat B) work C) name D) everyone
Answer: A. throat. The passage specifies throat: “… have feet, but they don't walk; Neither do they speak through their throat.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Their idols are ______ and gold, The work of men's hands.”? (115:4, WEB) Easy
A) ashes B) silver C) copper D) sand
Answer: B. silver. Psalms 115:4 uses silver: “Their idols are silver and gold, The work of men's hands.”

Psalms 117
Q1. Multiple choice: How does the reason for praise support the worldwide invitation? (117:1-2) Hard
A) It gives military strength as the only reason. B) It says only one household may worship. C) The LORD's enduring love and faithfulness are the reason all peoples are summoned to praise. D) It says God's faithfulness has ended.
Answer: C. The LORD's enduring love and faithfulness are the reason all peoples are summoned to praise.. Verse 1 addresses the nations; verse 2 supplies the reason. The short psalm connects God's character with worship beyond one ethnic community.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “For his loving kindness is great toward us. Yahweh's ______ endures forever. Praise Yah!” (117:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: faithfulness. The cited words are “For his loving kindness is great toward us. Yahweh's faithfulness endures forever. Praise Yah!”
Q3. True or false: Only Israel is addressed in the call to praise in this psalm. (117:1-2) Easy
Answer: False. The psalm calls all nations and peoples to praise the LORD because of His steadfast love and faithfulness. Its two verses make it the shortest psalm.

Psalms 137
Q1. True or false: The exiles wept by the rivers of Babylon. (137:1) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 137:1 records: “By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat down. Yes, we wept, when we remembered Zion.”
Q2. Multiple choice: How should the psalm's violent closing wish be distinguished from a direct command to its readers? (137:7-9) Hard
A) Every prayer automatically commands the reader to perform its imagery. B) The psalm was written during an uncomplicated celebration. C) The closing lines contain no anger. D) It is an exiled community's anguished appeal for judgment, not an instruction to enact revenge.
Answer: D. It is an exiled community's anguished appeal for judgment, not an instruction to enact revenge.. The language is morally disturbing and belongs to a lament shaped by devastation. Christian readers must also reckon with Jesus' command to love enemies; simply repeating the wish as a command ignores genre.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “How can we ______ Yahweh's song in a foreign ______?” (137:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: sing; land. The wording is “How can we sing Yahweh's song in a foreign land?” The missing terms belong in that order.

Raze it even to its foundation!"” (137:7); “Daughter of ______, doomed to destruction, He will be happy who rewards you, As you have served us.” (137:8, WEB) Hard
A) Babylon; Edom B) Edom; Tarshish C) Edom; Babylon D) Gilead; Babylon
Answer: C. Edom; Babylon. Verse 7 reads “Remember, Yahweh, against the children of Edom, The day of Jerusalem; Who said, "Raze it! Raze it even to its foundation!"”; verse 8 reads “Daughter of Babylon, doomed to destruction, He will be happy who rewards you, As you have served us.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Answer: False. The actual wording is “If I forget you, Jerusalem, Let my right hand forget its skill.” It says Jerusalem, not Zion.

Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Cause me to hear your loving ______ in the morning, For I trust in you. Cause me to know the way in which I should walk, …” (143:8); “______ me, Yahweh, from my enemies. I flee to you to hide me.” (143:9, WEB) Hard

Cause me to know the way in which I should walk, …”; verse 9 reads “Deliver me, Yahweh, from my enemies. I flee to you to hide me.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

A) heaven B) land C) garden D) valley
Answer: B. land. The passage specifies land: “… my hands to you. My soul thirsts for you, like a parched land. Selah.”
Q7. True or false: The psalmist asked God to teach him to do His will. (143:10) Easy
Answer: True. Psalms 143:10 records: “Teach me to do your will, For you are my God. Your Spirit is good. Lead me in the land of uprightness.”

Q4. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Answer: war. Psalms 144:1 records: “Blessed be Yahweh, my rock, Who teaches my hands to war, And my fingers to battle:”
Q6.
